Because: ✅ A0 gives analo…”

### Suggestions & Next Steps

- Power the ESP32 from a clean 3.3 V rail: ≥100 µF bulk + 100 nF ceramic at the VDD pins, and a separate regulator for servos/motors — brownouts masquerade as random firmware bugs.
- Respect boot strapping pins (GPIO 0/2/12 on classic ESP32, GPIO 2/8/9 on C3): no external pulls that fight the straps, and never drive GPIO12 high at reset on 5 V-tolerant modules.
- Enable the hardware watchdog (esp_task_wdt) around your main loop and use deep-sleep with RTC retention for battery nodes.
- Gate every BPM/SpO₂ output behind a perfusion threshold (MIN_IR_LEVEL) + signal-quality index and a 5–6 s hold; never publish raw beats to UI.
- Calibrate the IR/RED ratio against a reference pulse oximeter at rest and motion to derive your personal SpO₂ curve; ambient-light cancellation (dark current subtraction) is mandatory in daylight.
- Derive A/B/C Steinhart–Hart coefficients from three known temperature points with a calibrated reference (ice point, room temp, ~50 °C oil bath) rather than trusting datasheet nominal constants.
- Keep excitation current low (<100 µA) and average ADC over ≥16 samples to keep self-heating error below 0.1 °C for 100k NTC @ 25 °C.
- Lock in the winning decision from this chat into your project docs/repo before the next ChatGPT session; context window loss is the main failure mode.

STEP 8 — ESP32 CODE
Example:

#include <WiFi.h>
#include <WebSocketsServer.h>

const char* ssid = "YOUR_WIFI";
const char* password = "YOUR_PASS";

WebSocketsServer webSocket = WebSocketsServer(81);

void setup() {

  Serial.begin(115200);

  WiFi.begin(ssid, password);

  while(WiFi.status()!=WL_CONNECTED){
    delay(1000);
  }

  webSocket.begin();
}

void loop() {

  webSocket.loop();

  int hr = random(70,90);
  int spo2 = random(95,100);
  float temp = 36.5;

  String json =
  "{";
  json += "\"hr\":" + String(hr) + ",";
  json += "\"spo2\":" + String(spo2) + ",";
  json += "\"temp\":" + String(temp);
  json += "}";

  webSocket.broadcastTXT(json);

  delay(1000);
}
STEP 9 — REACT RECEIVE LIVE DATA
Inside React:

useEffect(() => {

  const ws = new WebSocket("ws://192.168.1.5:81");

  ws.onmessage = (event) => {

    const data = JSON.parse(event.data);

    setHeartRate(data.hr);
    setSpo2(data.spo2);
    setTemp(data.temp);
  };

}, []);
Now your dashboard updates LIVE.

STEP 10 — DUMP READINGS TO CSV
Inside React:

const exportCSV = () => {

  const rows = telemetryData.map(d =>
    `${d.time},${d.hr},${d.spo2},${d.temp}`
  );

  const csv =
    "Time,HR,SpO2,Temp\n" +
    rows.join("\n");

  const blob = new Blob([csv]);

  const a = document.createElement("a");

  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

  a.download = "patient_data.csv";

  a.click();
};

STEP 22 — ESP32 REALTIME CODE
Example:

#include <WiFi.h>
#include <WebSocketsServer.h>

const char* ssid = "YOUR_WIFI";
const char* password = "YOUR_PASS";

WebSocketsServer webSocket = WebSocketsServer(81);

void setup() {

  Serial.begin(115200);

  WiFi.begin(ssid,password);

  while(WiFi.status()!=WL_CONNECTED){
    delay(1000);
  }

  webSocket.begin();
}

void loop() {

  webSocket.loop();

  int hr = random(70,90);
  int spo2 = random(95,100);
  float temp = 36.5;
  int rr = 16;

  String json = "{";
  json += "\"hr\":" + String(hr) + ",";
  json += "\"spo2\":" + String(spo2) + ",";
  json += "\"temp\":" + String(temp) + ",";
  json += "\"rr\":" + String(rr);
  json += "}";

  webSocket.broadcastTXT(json);

  delay(1000);
}
Upload.

useEffect(() => {

  const ws = new WebSocket("ws://192.168.1.5:81");

  ws.onmessage = (event) => {

    const data = JSON.parse(event.data);

    setHr(data.hr);
    setSpo2(data.spo2);
    setTemp(data.temp);
    setRr(data.rr);
  };

}, []);
NOW:
dashboard updates live.

Humanity approves. Briefly.

PHASE 10 — STORE HISTORY
STEP 25 — SAVE READINGS
Create array:

const [history,setHistory] = useState([]);
Add data:

setHistory(prev=>[
  ...prev,
  data
]);
PHASE 11 — EXPORT CSV
STEP 26 — CREATE EXPORT BUTTON
<button onClick={exportCSV}>
Export CSV
</button>
STEP 27 — EXPORT FUNCTION
const exportCSV = () => {

  const rows = history.map(d =>
    `${d.hr},${d.spo2},${d.temp},${d.rr}`
  );

  const csv =
  "HR,SpO2,Temp,RR\n" +
  rows.join("\n");

  const blob = new Blob([csv]);

  const a = document.createElement("a");

  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

  a.download = "patient_data.csv";

  a.click();
};
Now it downloads CSV.

STEP 18 — SEND SENSOR DATA TO DASHBOARD
Now we add WiFi.

Paste this:

#include <WiFi.h>

const char* ssid = "YOUR_WIFI";
const char* password = "YOUR_PASSWORD";

WiFiServer server(80);

void setup() {

  Serial.begin(115200);

  WiFi.begin(ssid,password);

  while(WiFi.status()!=WL_CONNECTED){
    delay(1000);
  }

  Serial.println(WiFi.localIP());

  server.begin();
}

void loop() {

  WiFiClient client = server.available();

  if(client){

    String json =
    "{\"hr\":78,\"spo2\":98,\"temp\":36.7}";

    client.println("HTTP/1.1 200 OK");
    client.println("Content-Type: application/json");
    client.println();
    client.println(json);

    client.stop();
  }
}
Upload.

STEP 19 — FIND ESP32 IP
Open Serial Monitor.

192.168.1.5

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

function App() {

  const [data,setData] = useState({
    hr:0,
    spo2:0,
    temp:0
  });

  useEffect(()=>{

    setInterval(async()=>{

      const res = await fetch("http://192.168.1.5");

      const json = await res.json();

      setData(json);

    },1000);

  },[]);

  return (

    <div>

      <h1>VI TRAK 637</h1>

      <h2>HR: {data.hr}</h2>

      <h2>SpO2: {data.spo2}</h2>

      <h2>Temp: {data.temp}</h2>

    </div>
  );
}

export default App;

import { HeartPulse, Activity, Thermometer, Wind } from "lucide-react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";

function App() {

  const patients = [

    {name:"Ram",hr:72,spo2:98,temp:36.6,rr:16,status:"Stable"},
    {name:"Vijay",hr:128,spo2:89,temp:38.7,rr:28,status:"Critical"},
    {name:"Tarun",hr:92,spo2:93,temp:37.8,rr:20,status:"Watch"},
    {name:"Neeraj",hr:68,spo2:99,temp:36.5,rr:14,status:"Good"},
    {name:"Nikhil",hr:104,spo2:92,temp:37.6,rr:22,status:"Watch"},

    {name:"Pavan",hr:75,spo2:97,temp:36.7,rr:17,status:"Stable"},
    {name:"Abdul",hr:132,spo2:85,temp:39.2,rr:30,status:"Critical"},
    {name:"Samuel",hr:70,spo2:98,temp:36.4,rr:15,status:"Good"},
    {name:"Taj",hr:100,spo2:91,temp:37.9,rr:24,status:"Watch"},
    {name:"Krishna",hr:74,spo2:97,temp:36.6,rr:16,status:"Stable"},
  ];

  const getColor = (status:string)=>{

    if(status==="Critical") return "#ff4d6d";

    if(status==="Watch") return "#ff9800";

    return "#35c56a";
  }

  return (

    <div
      style={{
        minHeight:"100vh",
        padding:"40px",
        background:
        "linear-gradient(to bottom right,#f5f1f8,#efe8ff)",

        fontFamily:"Inter,sans-serif"
      }}
    >

      <motion.div
        initial={{opacity:0,y:-30}}
        animate={{opacity:1,y:0}}
        transition={{duration:1}}
      >

        <h1
          style={{
            textAlign:"center",
            fontSize:"64px",
            color:"#7b3fe4",
            marginBottom:"0px",
            fontWeight:"800",
            letterSpacing:"2px"
          }}
        >
          VI TRAK 637
        </h1>

        <p
          style={{
            textAlign:"center",
            color:"#666",
            fontSize:"22px",
            marginBottom:"50px"
          }}
        >
          Advanced Human Bio-Analytics Engine
        </p>

      </motion.div>

      <div
        style={{
          display:"grid",
          gridTemplateColumns:"repeat(5,1fr)",
          gap:"25px"
        }}
      >

        {patients.map((p,index)=>(

          <motion.div

            whileHover={{
              y:-10,
              scale:1.03
            }}

            key={index}

            style={{
              background:"rgba(255,255,255,0.55)",
              backdropFilter:"blur(20px)",
              borderRadius:"30px",
              padding:"25px",

              boxShadow:
              "0 8px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.08)",

              border:
              "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.5)"
            }}
          >

            <div
              style={{
                display:"flex",
                justifyContent:"space-between",
                alignItems:"center",
                marginBottom:"20px"
              }}
            >

              <h2
                style={{
                  margin:0,
                  color:"#222"
                }}
              >
                {p.name}
              </h2>

              <div
                style={{
                  background:getColor(p.status),
                  color:"white",
                  padding:"6px 14px",
                  borderRadius:"50px",
                  fontSize:"12px"
                }}
              >
                {p.status}
              </div>

            </div>

            <div
              style={{
                display:"flex",
                flexDirection:"column",
                gap:"18px"
              }}
            >

              <Metric
                icon={<HeartPulse color="#c56cf0"/>}
                label="HR"
                value={`${p.hr} BPM`}
              />

              <Metric
                icon={<Wind color="#4d96ff"/>}
                label="RR"
                value={`${p.rr} /min`}
              />

              <Metric
                icon={<Activity color="#35c56a"/>}
                label="SpO₂"
                value={`${p.spo2}%`}
              />

              <Metric
                icon={<Thermometer color="#ff9800"/>}
                label="Temp"
                value={`${p.temp}°C`}
              />

            </div>

            <div
              style={{
                marginTop:"20px",
                padding:"12px",
                borderRadius:"16px",

                background:
                "rgba(255,255,255,0.4)",

                color:getColor(p.status),

                fontWeight:"600"
              }}
            >
              {p.status==="Critical"
              ? "⚠ Immediate Attention Needed"

              : p.status==="Watch"
              ? "⚠ Under Observation"

              : "✓ No Alerts"}
            </div>

          </motion.div>

        ))}

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

function Metric({icon,label,value}:any){

  return(

    <div
      style={{
        display:"flex",
        justifyContent:"space-between",
        alignItems:"center"
      }}
    >

      <div
        style={{
          display:"flex",
          alignItems:"center",
          gap:"10px"
        }}
      >

        {icon}

        <span
          style={{
            color:"#555",
            fontWeight:"600"
          }}
        >
          {label}
        </span>

      </div>

      <span
        style={{
          fontWeight:"700",
          color:"#222"
        }}
      >
        {value}
      </span>

    </div>
  )
}

export default App;

```tsx
import { HeartPulse, Activity, Thermometer, Wind } from "lucide-react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";

function App() {

  const patients = [

    {
      name: "Ram",
      hr: 72,
      spo2: 98,
      temp: 36.6,
      rr: 16,
      status: "Stable"
    },

    },

      status: "Good"
    },

    {

    },

      rr: 17,
      status: "Stable"
    },

    {
      name: "Abdul",
      hr: 132,
      spo2: 85,
      temp: 39.2,
      rr: 30,
      status: "Critical"
    },

    {
      name: "Samuel",
      hr: 70,
      spo2: 98,
      temp: 36.4,
      rr: 15,
      status: "Good"
    },

    {
      name: "Taj",
      hr: 100,
      spo2: 91,
      temp: 37.9,
      rr: 24,
      status: "Watch"
    },

    {
      name: "Krishna",
      hr: 74,
      spo2: 97,
      temp: 36.6,
      rr: 16,
      status: "Stable"
    },
  ];

  const getColor = (status: string) => {

    if (status === "Critical") return "#ff4d6d";

    if (status === "Watch") return "#ff9800";

    if (status === "Good") return "#35c56a";

    return "#6c63ff";
  };

  return (

    <div
      style={{
        minHeight: "100vh",
        padding: "40px",
        background:
          "linear-gradient(to bottom right,#0f172a,#111827,#1e1b4b)",

        fontFamily: "Inter, sans-serif"
      }}
    >

      <motion.div
        initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -40 }}
        animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
        transition={{ duration: 1 }}
      >

        <h1
          style={{
            textAlign: "center",
            fontSize: "64px",
            color: "#c084fc",
            marginBottom: "10px",
            fontWeight: "900",
            letterSpacing: "3px"
          }}
        >
          VI TRAK 637
        </h1>

        <p
          style={{
            textAlign: "center",
            color: "#cbd5e1",
            fontSize: "22px",
            marginBottom: "50px"
          }}
        >
          Advanced Human Bio-Analytics Monitoring System
        </p>

      </motion.div>

      <div
        style={{
          display: "grid",
          gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(auto-fit,minmax(300px,1fr))",
          gap: "25px"
        }}
      >

        {patients.map((p, index) => (

          <motion.div

            key={index}

            whileHover={{
              y: -10,
              scale: 1.03
            }}

            transition={{
              type: "spring",
              stiffness: 200
            }}

            style={{
              background: "rgba(255,255,255,0.08)",
              backdropFilter: "blur(20px)",
              borderRadius: "30px",
              padding: "25px",

              border:
                "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1)",

              boxShadow:
                "0 8px 32px rgba(0,0,0,0.35)"
            }}
          >

            <div
              style={{
                display: "flex",
                justifyContent: "space-between",
                alignItems: "center",
                marginBottom: "25px"
              }}
            >

              <h2
                style={{
                  margin: 0,
                  color: "white",
                  fontSize: "24px"
                }}
              >
                {p.name}
              </h2>

              <div
                style={{
                  background: getColor(p.status),
                  color: "white",
                  padding: "6px 16px",
                  borderRadius: "50px",
                  fontSize: "12px",
                  fontWeight: "700"
                }}
              >
                {p.status}
              </div>

            </div>

            <div
              style={{
                display: "flex",
                flexDirection: "column",
                gap: "18px"
              }}
            >

              <Metric
                icon={<HeartPulse color="#ff4d6d" />}
                label="Heart Rate"
                value={`${p.hr} BPM`}
              />

              <Metric
                icon={<Activity color="#35c56a" />}
                label="SpO₂"
                value={`${p.spo2}%`}
              />

              <Metric
                icon={<Thermometer color="#ff9800" />}
                label="Temperature"
                value={`${p.temp}°C`}
              />

              <Metric
                icon={<Wind color="#4d96ff" />}
                label="Respiration"
                value={`${p.rr} /min`}
              />

            </div>

            <div
              style={{
                marginTop: "25px",
                padding: "14px",
                borderRadius: "18px",

                background:
                  "rgba(255,255,255,0.06)",

                color: getColor(p.status),

                fontWeight: "700",

                textAlign: "center"
              }}
            >

              {p.status === "Critical"
                ? "⚠ Immediate Medical Attention Needed"

                : p.status === "Watch"
                  ? "⚠ Under Observation"

                  : "✓ Stable Condition"}

            </div>

          </motion.div>

        ))}

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

function Metric({
  icon,
  label,
  value
}: any) {

  return (

    <div
      style={{
        display: "flex",
        justifyContent: "space-between",
        alignItems: "center",

        background:
          "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)",

        padding: "14px",
        borderRadius: "18px"
      }}
    >

      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          gap: "12px"
        }}
      >

        {icon}

        <span
          style={{
            color: "#cbd5e1",
            fontWeight: "600"
          }}
        >
          {label}
        </span>

      </div>

      <span
        style={{
          fontWeight: "800",
          color: "white",
          fontSize: "18px"
        }}
      >
        {value}
      </span>

    </div>
  );
}

export default App;
```

```tsx id="v4z8hf"
            {/* ALERT */}

            <div
              style={{
                marginTop: "24px",

                background:
                  "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)",

                borderRadius: "20px",

                padding: "16px",

                textAlign: "center",

                color:
                  getStatusColor(p.status),

                fontWeight: "700"
              }}
            >

              {p.status === "Critical"
                ? "⚠ Immediate Medical Attention"

                : p.status === "Watch"
                  ? "⚠ Under Observation"

                  : "✓ Stable Condition"}

            </div>

          </motion.div>

        ))}

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

/* METRIC CARD */

function Metric({
  icon,
  label,
  value
}: any) {

  return (

    <div
      style={{
        display: "flex",
        justifyContent: "space-between",
        alignItems: "center",

        background:
          "rgba(255,255,255,0.04)",

        padding: "15px",

        borderRadius: "18px"
      }}
    >

      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          gap: "12px"
        }}
      >

        {icon}

        <span
          style={{
            color: "#d1d5db",
            fontWeight: "600"
          }}
        >
          {label}
        </span>

      </div>

      <span
        style={{
          color: "white",
          fontWeight: "800",
          fontSize: "18px"
        }}
      >
        {value}
      </span>

    </div>
  );
}

/* TOP STAT CARD */

function StatCard({
  title,
  value,
  color
}: any) {

  return (

    <motion.div

      whileHover={{
        scale: 1.03
      }}

      style={{

        background:
          "rgba(255,255,255,0.08)",

        border:
          "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08)",

        backdropFilter: "blur(20px)",

        borderRadius: "28px",

        padding: "24px",

        boxShadow:
          "0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.2)"
      }}
    >

      <p
        style={{
          color: "#9ca3af",
          marginTop: 0,
          marginBottom: "12px"
        }}
      >
        {title}
      </p>

      <h2
        style={{
          color,
          margin: 0,
          fontSize: "36px",
          fontWeight: "900"
        }}
      >
        {value}
      </h2>

    </motion.div>
  );
}

/* ICON BUTTON */

function GlassIcon({ icon }: any) {

  return (

    <div
      style={{

        width: "58px",
        height: "58px",

        borderRadius: "20px",

        background:
          "rgba(255,255,255,0.08)",

        display: "flex",

        justifyContent: "center",

        alignItems: "center",

        border:
          "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08)",

        backdropFilter: "blur(20px)"
      }}
    >

      {icon}

    </div>
  );
}

export default App;
```

NOT charts.

- smoother
- aesthetic
- faster

```tsx id="t7h3mv"
    L45 8

    L65 10

            />
```

```tsx id="k4o8yx"
              icon={<HeartPulse color="#a855f7" />}
              label="PR"

                }}
              >

                {p.status === "Critical"
                  ? <AlertTriangle size={18} />

                  : <ShieldCheck size={18} />
                }

              <div
                style={{
                  color:
                    getStatusColor(p.status),

                  fontWeight: "600"
                }}

                  ? "High Fever"

                  : "No Alerts"}
              </div>

        ))}

      </div>

  );
}

function Metric({
  icon,
  label,
  value,
  color
}: any) {

  return (

    <div
      style={{
        display: "flex",

        justifyContent: "space-between",

        alignItems: "center",

        padding: "12px 0",

        borderBottom:
          "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.05)"
      }}
    >

      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          gap: "10px"
        }}
      >

        {icon}

        <span
          style={{
            color: "#555",
            fontWeight: "600"
          }}
        >
          {label}
        </span>

      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          gap: "18px"
        }}
      >

        <span
          style={{
            fontWeight: "700",
            color: "#111"
          }}
        >
          {value}
        </span>

        <Wave color={color} />

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

function Wave({ color }: any) {

  return (

    <svg width="90" height="24">

      <path
        d="
        M0 12
        L10 12
        L15 4
        L20 18
        L25 10
        L35 12
        L45 6
        L55 16
        L65 10
        L75 13
        L90 12
        "

        stroke={color}

        strokeWidth="2"

        fill="none"

        strokeLinecap="round"
      />

    </svg>
  );
}

export default App;
```

```tsx id="qtf2n8"
  opacity: 0,
  y: 30
}}

  opacity: 1,
  y: 0
}}

transition={{
  duration: 0.6,
  delay: index * 0.08
}}

whileHover={{
  y: -10,
  scale: 1.02
}}
```

```tsx id="4l5s2x"
  }}
/>
```

```tsx
function Metric({
  icon,
  label,
  value,
  color,
}: any) {
  return (
    <motion.div
      whileHover={{
        x: 4,
      }}
      transition={{
        type: "spring",
        stiffness: 200,
      }}
      style={{
        display: "flex",
        justifyContent: "space-between",
        alignItems: "center",
        padding: "12px 0",
        borderBottom: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.04)",
      }}
    >
      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          gap: "10px",
        }}
      >
        {icon}

        <span
          style={{
            color: "#475569",
            fontWeight: 600,
          }}
        >
          {label}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          gap: "12px",
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            fontWeight: 700,
            color: "#111827",
          }}
        >
          {value}
        </div>

        <motion.svg
          width="90"
          height="24"
          animate={{
            x: [0, 2, 0],
          }}
          transition={{
            repeat: Infinity,
            duration: 1.2,
          }}
        >
          <path
            d="M0 12 L8 12 L12 4 L18 20 L24 8 L30 12 L36 10 L42 14 L48 6 L54 12 L60 9 L66 15 L72 11 L78 13 L84 8 L90 12"
            fill="none"
            stroke={color}
            strokeWidth="2"
            strokeLinecap="round"
          />
        </motion.svg>
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

export default App;
```

```tsx
function SidebarIcon({
  icon,
  active = false,
}: any) {
  return (
    <motion.div
      whileHover={{
        scale: 1.08,
        y: -2,
      }}
      whileTap={{
        scale: 0.95,
      }}
      style={{
        width: "62px",
        height: "62px",
        borderRadius: "22px",
        background: active
          ? "linear-gradient(135deg,#a855f7,#7c3aed)"
          : "rgba(255,255,255,0.55)",

        display: "flex",
        alignItems: "center",
        justifyContent: "center",

        color: active ? "white" : "#475569",

        boxShadow: active
          ? "0 12px 30px rgba(124,58,237,0.35)"
          : "none",
      }}
    >
      {icon}
    </motion.div>
  );
}

function StatCard({
  title,
  value,
  color,
}: any) {
  return (
    <motion.div
      whileHover={{
        y: -6,
        scale: 1.02,
      }}
      initial={{
        opacity: 0,
        y: 20,
      }}
      animate={{
        opacity: 1,
        y: 0,
      }}
      style={{
        background: "rgba(255,255,255,0.65)",
        backdropFilter: "blur(28px)",
        borderRadius: "28px",
        padding: "24px",
        border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.6)",
        boxShadow: "0 12px 35px rgba(0,0,0,0.04)",
      }}
    >
      <div
        style={{
          color: "#64748b",
          fontWeight: 600,
          fontSize: "15px",
        }}
      >
        {title}
      </div>

      <motion.div
        animate={{
          opacity: [0.8, 1, 0.8],
        }}
        transition={{
          repeat: Infinity,
          duration: 3,
        }}
        style={{
          marginTop: "14px",
          fontSize: "46px",
          fontWeight: 800,
          color,
        }}
      >
        {value}
      </motion.div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

function PatientCard({
  patient,
  index,
}: any) {
  const getStatusColor = () => {
    if (patient.status === "Critical")
      return "#ef4444";

    if (patient.status === "Watch")
      return "#f59e0b";

    return "#22c55e";
  };

  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{
        opacity: 0,
        y: 40,
      }}
      animate={{
        opacity: 1,
        y: 0,
      }}
      exit={{
        opacity: 0,
      }}
      transition={{
        duration: 0.6,
        delay: index * 0.08,
      }}
      whileHover={{
        y: -10,
        scale: 1.015,
      }}
      style={{
        background: "rgba(255,255,255,0.68)",
        backdropFilter: "blur(32px)",
        borderRadius: "34px",
        padding: "22px",
        border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.7)",
        boxShadow:
          "0 15px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.04)",
      }}
    >
      {/* TOP */}

      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          justifyContent: "space-between",
          alignItems: "center",
          marginBottom: "22px",
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            display: "flex",
            gap: "14px",
            alignItems: "center",
          }}
        >
          <motion.div
            whileHover={{
              rotate: 8,
            }}
            style={{
              width: "62px",
              height: "62px",
              borderRadius: "22px",
              background:
                "linear-gradient(135deg,#ede9fe,#ddd6fe)",
              display: "flex",
              alignItems: "center",
              justifyContent: "center",
              color: "#7c3aed",
              fontWeight: 700,
              fontSize: "22px",
            }}
          >
            {patient.name[0]}
          </motion.div>

          <div>
            <h2
              style={{
                margin: 0,
                fontSize: "24px",
              }}
            >
              {patient.name}
            </h2>

            <p
              style={{
                margin: "6px 0 0",
                color: "#64748b",
              }}
            >
              {patient.ward}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <motion.div
          animate={{
            opacity: [0.7, 1, 0.7],
          }}
          transition={{
            repeat: Infinity,
            duration: 2,
          }}
          style={{
            background: `${getStatusColor()}15`,
            color: getStatusColor(),
            padding: "10px 16px",
            borderRadius: "999px",
            fontSize: "12px",
            fontWeight: 700,
          }}
        >
          {patient.status}
        </motion.div>
      </div>

      {/* METRICS */}

      <Metric
        icon={<HeartPulse color="#d946ef" />}
        label="Heart Rate"
        value={`${patient.hr} BPM`}
        color="#d946ef"
      />

      <Metric
        icon={<Wind color="#2563eb" />}
        label="Respiration"
        value={`${patient.rr} /min`}
        color="#2563eb"
      />

      <Metric
        icon={<Activity color="#22c55e" />}
        label="SpO₂"
        value={`${patient.spo2}%`}
        color="#22c55e"
      />

      <Metric
        icon={<Thermometer color="#f97316" />}
        label="Temperature"
        value={`${patient.temp}°C`}
        color="#f97316"
      />

      {/* FOOTER */}

      <motion.div
        whileHover={{
          scale: 1.01,
        }}
        style={{
          marginTop: "20px",
          padding: "16px",
          borderRadius: "22px",
          background:
            "rgba(255,255,255,0.55)",

          display: "flex",
          justifyContent: "space-between",
          alignItems: "center",
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            gap: "8px",
            color: getStatusColor(),
            fontWeight: 700,
          }}
        >
          <ShieldCheck size={16} />
          {patient.status}
        </div>

        <motion.div
          whileHover={{
            x: 3,
          }}
          style={{
            display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            gap: "4px",
            color: "#64748b",
            fontWeight: 600,
          }}
        >
          Details
          <ChevronRight size={16} />
        </motion.div>
      </motion.div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

function Metric({
  icon,
  label,
  value,
  color,
}: any) {
  return (
    <motion.div
      whileHover={{
        x: 4,
      }}
      transition={{
        type: "spring",
        stiffness: 300,
      }}
      style={{
        display: "flex",
        justifyContent: "space-between",
        alignItems: "center",
        padding: "16px 0",
        borderBottom:
          "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.04)",
      }}
    >
      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          gap: "12px",
        }}
      >
        {icon}

        <span
          style={{
            color: "#475569",
            fontWeight: 600,
          }}
        >
          {label}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          gap: "12px",
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            fontWeight: 700,
            color: "#0f172a",
          }}
        >
          {value}
        </div>

        <motion.svg
          width="90"
          height="24"
          animate={{
            x: [0, 2, 0],
          }}
          transition={{
            repeat: Infinity,
            duration: 1.2,
          }}
        >
          <path
            d="M0 12 L8 12 L12 4 L18 20 L24 8 L30 12 L36 10 L42 14 L48 6 L54 12 L60 9 L66 15 L72 11 L78 13 L84 8 L90 12"
            fill="none"
            stroke={color}
            strokeWidth="2"
            strokeLinecap="round"
          />
        </motion.svg>
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

export default App;
```

```tsx
                icon={<HeartPulse color="#d946ef" />}
                label="Average HR"
                value="82 BPM"
              />

              <Metric
                icon={<Activity color="#22c55e" />}
                label="Average SpO₂"
                value="97%"
              />

              <Metric
                icon={<Thermometer color="#f97316" />}
                label="Temperature"
                value="36.8°C"
              />

              <Metric
                icon={<Wind color="#3b82f6" />}
                label="Respiration"
                value="18/min"
              />
            </div>
          </GlassCard>

          {/* ALERT */}

          <motion.div
            whileHover={{
              scale: 1.02,
            }}
            style={{
              height: "220px",
              borderRadius: "36px",
              background:
                "linear-gradient(135deg,#ef4444,#dc2626)",
              padding: "30px",
              color: "white",
              boxShadow:
                "0 20px 60px rgba(239,68,68,0.35)",
            }}
          >
            <div
              style={{
                fontSize: "18px",
                opacity: 0.8,
              }}
            >
              Emergency Alert
            </div>

            <h1
              style={{
                fontSize: "42px",
                marginTop: "14px",
              }}
            >
              2 Critical Patients
            </h1>

            <div
              style={{
                marginTop: "20px",
                opacity: 0.85,
                lineHeight: 1.6,
              }}
            >
              Immediate intervention required for
              abnormal respiratory and cardiac
              readings.
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function FloatingButton({ icon }: any) {
  return (
    <motion.div
      whileHover={{
        scale: 1.08,
        y: -2,
      }}
      style={{
        width: "60px",
        height: "60px",
        borderRadius: "22px",
        background: "rgba(255,255,255,0.65)",
        display: "flex",
        alignItems: "center",
        justifyContent: "center",
      }}
    >
      {icon}
    </motion.div>
  );
}

function GlassCard({
  children,
  height,
}: any) {
  return (
    <motion.div
      whileHover={{
        y: -4,
      }}
      style={{
        height,
        borderRadius: "36px",
        background: "rgba(255,255,255,0.55)",
        backdropFilter: "blur(28px)",
        border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.5)",
        padding: "28px",
        boxShadow: "0 10px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)",
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </motion.div>
  );
}

function Metric({
  icon,
  label,
  value,
}: any) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: "flex",
        justifyContent: "space-between",
        alignItems: "center",
      }}
    >
      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          gap: "12px",
        }}
      >
        {icon}

        <div
          style={{
            fontWeight: 600,
          }}
        >
          {label}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          fontWeight: 700,
          fontSize: "18px",
        }}
      >
        {value}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function PatientCard({ patient }: any) {
  const getColor = () => {
    if (patient.status === "Critical")
      return "#ef4444";

    if (patient.status === "Watch")
      return "#f59e0b";

    return "#22c55e";
  };

  return (
    <motion.div
      whileHover={{
        y: -8,
        scale: 1.02,
      }}
      style={{
        borderRadius: "34px",
        background: "rgba(255,255,255,0.58)",
        backdropFilter: "blur(28px)",
        border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.5)",
        padding: "24px",
        boxShadow: "0 10px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.04)",
      }}
    >
      {/* TOP */}

      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          justifyContent: "space-between",
          alignItems: "center",
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            gap: "14px",
          }}
        >
          <div
            style={{
              width: "58px",
              height: "58px",
              borderRadius: "20px",
              background:
                "linear-gradient(135deg,#ddd6fe,#ede9fe)",
              display: "flex",
              alignItems: "center",
              justifyContent: "center",
              fontWeight: 700,
              color: "#7c3aed",
              fontSize: "22px",
            }}
          >
            {patient.name[0]}
          </div>

          <div>
            <h2
              style={{
                margin: 0,
              }}
            >
              {patient.name}
            </h2>

            <div
              style={{
                color: "#6b7280",
                marginTop: "4px",
              }}
            >
              Live Monitoring
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            background: `${getColor()}20`,
            color: getColor(),
            padding: "8px 14px",
            borderRadius: "999px",
            fontSize: "12px",
            fontWeight: 700,
          }}
        >
          {patient.status}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* METRICS */}

      <div
        style={{
          marginTop: "26px",
          display: "flex",
          flexDirection: "column",
          gap: "18px",
        }}
      >
        <PatientMetric
          icon={<HeartPulse color="#d946ef" />}
          label="Heart Rate"
          value={`${patient.hr} BPM`}
        />

        <PatientMetric
          icon={<Activity color="#22c55e" />}
          label="SpO₂"
          value={`${patient.spo2}%`}
        />

        <PatientMetric
          icon={<Thermometer color="#f97316" />}
          label="Temp"
          value={`${patient.temp}°C`}
        />

        <PatientMetric
          icon={<Wind color="#3b82f6" />}
          label="Respiration"
          value={`${patient.rr}/min`}
        />
      </div>

      {/* ECG */}

      <motion.svg
        width="100%"
        height="70"
        style={{
          marginTop: "24px",
        }}
      >
        <motion.path
          d="M0 35 L30 35 L45 12 L70 60 L95 20 L120 35 L150 35 L180 15 L220 50 L260 35 L320 35"
          fill="none"
          stroke={getColor()}
          strokeWidth="3"
          strokeLinecap="round"
          animate={{
            pathLength: [0, 1],
          }}
          transition={{
            repeat: Infinity,
            duration: 2,
          }}
        />
      </motion.svg>

      {/* FOOTER */}

      <div
        style={{
          marginTop: "10px",
          padding: "14px",
          borderRadius: "20px",
          background: "rgba(255,255,255,0.5)",
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          gap: "10px",
          color: getColor(),
          fontWeight: 700,
        }}
      >
        <ShieldCheck size={18} />

        {patient.status === "Critical"
          ? "Immediate Attention Required"
          : patient.status === "Watch"
          ? "Patient Under Observation"
          : "Vitals Stable"}
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

function PatientMetric({
  icon,
  label,
  value,
}: any) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: "flex",
        justifyContent: "space-between",
        alignItems: "center",
      }}
    >
      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          gap: "12px",
        }}
      >
        {icon}

        <div
          style={{
            color: "#4b5563",
            fontWeight: 600,
          }}
        >
          {label}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          fontWeight: 700,
          color: "#111827",
        }}
      >
        {value}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default App;
```

```tsx
import { motion } from "framer-motion";

export default function IntroScreen() {

  return (

    <motion.div

      initial={{opacity:1}}
      exit={{opacity:0}}

      style={{
        height:"100vh",
        width:"100%",
        background:"#F4EEF7",
        display:"flex",
        justifyContent:"center",
        alignItems:"center",
        position:"fixed",
        zIndex:9999,
        overflow:"hidden"
      }}
    >

      <motion.div

        animate={{
          scale:[1,1.05,1],
          y:[0,-10,0]
        }}

        transition={{
          repeat:Infinity,
          duration:6
        }}

        style={{
          width:"500px",
          height:"500px",
          borderRadius:"50%",
          background:"rgba(255,255,255,0.5)",
          backdropFilter:"blur(30px)",
          display:"flex",
          justifyContent:"center",
          alignItems:"center",
          flexDirection:"column",
          boxShadow:"0 20px 80px rgba(203,108,230,0.3)"
        }}
      >

        <motion.div

          animate={{
            scale:[1,1.08,1]
          }}

          transition={{
            repeat:Infinity,
            duration:1.5
          }}

          style={{
            width:"180px",
            height:"180px",
            borderRadius:"50%",
            border:"12px solid #CB6CE6",
            display:"flex",
            justifyContent:"center",
            alignItems:"center",
            fontSize:"70px",
            color:"#CB6CE6",
            background:"white"
          }}
        >
          ❤️
        </motion.div>

        <h1
          style={{
            marginTop:"40px",
            fontSize:"64px",
            fontFamily:"Orbitron",
            color:"#7A36F0"
          }}
        >
          VI-TRACK
        </h1>

        <p
          style={{
            marginTop:"15px",
            color:"#666",
            fontSize:"20px"
          }}
        >
          Continuous Intelligent Health Monitoring
        </p>

        <motion.div

          initial={{width:0}}

          animate={{width:"300px"}}

          transition={{
            duration:4
          }}

          style={{
            marginTop:"40px",
            height:"12px",
            borderRadius:"20px",
            background:"linear-gradient(to right,#CB6CE6,#E8B4FF)"
          }}
        />

      </motion.div>

    </motion.div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { patients } from "../data/patients";
import {
HeartPulse,
Thermometer,
Wind,
Activity
} from "lucide-react";

import { motion } from "framer-motion";

export default function BioDashboard(){

return(

<div
style={{
minHeight:"100vh",
background:"#F4EEF7",
padding:"40px"
}}
>

{/* TOPBAR */}

<div
style={{
display:"flex",
justifyContent:"space-between",
alignItems:"center",
marginBottom:"40px"
}}
>

<div>

<h1
style={{
fontSize:"48px",
fontFamily:"Orbitron",
color:"#7A36F0"
}}
>
VI-TRACK 637
</h1>

<p
style={{
color:"#666",
marginTop:"10px"
}}
>
Advanced Bio Monitoring Dashboard
</p>

</div>

<div
style={{
padding:"14px 24px",
borderRadius:"40px",
background:"rgba(255,255,255,0.6)",
backdropFilter:"blur(20px)",
display:"flex",
gap:"10px",
alignItems:"center"
}}
>
<div
style={{
width:"10px",
height:"10px",
borderRadius:"50%",
background:"#34C56B"
}}
/>

<span>Live Monitoring Active</span>

</div>

</div>

{/* PATIENT GRID */}

<div
style={{
display:"grid",
gridTemplateColumns:"repeat(5,1fr)",
gap:"25px"
}}
>

{patients.map((p,index)=>(

<motion.div

whileHover={{
y:-10,
scale:1.03
}}

key={index}

style={{
background:"rgba(255,255,255,0.6)",
backdropFilter:"blur(20px)",
borderRadius:"30px",
padding:"25px",
boxShadow:"0 10px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.08)"
}}
>

<div
style={{
display:"flex",
justifyContent:"space-between",
alignItems:"center"
}}
>

<div>

<h2>{p.name}</h2>

<p
style={{
color:"#777",
marginTop:"5px"
}}
>
{p.room}
</p>

</div>

<div
style={{
padding:"8px 16px",
borderRadius:"40px",
background:
p.status==="Critical"
? "#ff4d6d"
: p.status==="Observation"
? "#ff9800"
: "#34C56B",

color:"white",
fontSize:"13px"
}}
>
{p.status}
</div>

</div>

<div
style={{
marginTop:"25px",
display:"flex",
flexDirection:"column",
gap:"20px"
}}
>

<Metric
icon={<HeartPulse color="#CB6CE6"/>}
label="Heart Rate"
value={`${p.hr} BPM`}
/>

<Metric
icon={<Activity color="#34C56B"/>}
label="SpO₂"
value={`${p.spo2}%`}
/>

<Metric
icon={<Thermometer color="#ff9800"/>}
label="Temperature"
value={`${p.temp}°C`}
/>

<Metric
icon={<Wind color="#4d96ff"/>}
label="Respiration"
value={`${p.rr} RPM`}
/>

</div>

</motion.div>

))}

</div>

</div>

)
}

function Metric({icon,label,value}:any){

return(

<div
style={{
display:"flex",
justifyContent:"space-between",
alignItems:"center"
}}
>

<div
style={{
display:"flex",
alignItems:"center",
gap:"12px"
}}
>

{icon}

<span
style={{
fontWeight:"600",
color:"#555"
}}
>
{label}
</span>

</div>

<span
style={{
fontWeight:"800",
fontSize:"20px"
}}
>
{value}
</span>

</div>

)

}
```

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import IntroScreen from "./components/IntroScreen";
import BioDashboard from "./components/BioDashboard";

function App(){

const [loading,setLoading] = useState(true);

useEffect(()=>{

setTimeout(()=>{

setLoading(false);

},5000);

},[]);

return(

<>

{loading
? <IntroScreen/>
: <BioDashboard/>
}

</>

)

}

export default App;
```

# STEP 4 — REPLACE `App.tsx`

```text id="tob3mh"

```

DELETE EVERYTHING.

PASTE:

```tsx id="jlwm2d"
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

import IntroScreen from "./components/IntroScreen";
import BioDashboard from "./components/BioDashboard";

function App(){

const [loading,setLoading] = useState(true);

useEffect(()=>{

setTimeout(()=>{

setLoading(false);

},5000);

},[]);

return(

<>

{loading
? <IntroScreen/>
: <BioDashboard/>
}

</>

)

}

export default App;
```

```tsx id="nxyv64"
            "0 20px 80px rgba(203,108,230,0.25)",
        }}
      >

        {/* LOGO */}

        <motion.div

          animate={{
            scale: [1, 1.06, 1],
          }}

          transition={{
            repeat: Infinity,
            duration: 2,
          }}

          style={{
            width: "180px",

          VI-TRACK
        </h1>

        <div
          style={{

            borderRadius: "20px",

              width: 0,
```

# YOUR MAIN PROBLEM

You pasted malformed JSX with broken spacing like:

```tsx id="n2p8c4"
style= {{
```

instead of:

```tsx id="b5v1k9"
style={{
```

AND:

```tsx id="m4z7x0"
< motion.div
```

instead of:

```tsx id="f3t2q8"
<motion.div
```

The formatter exploded during paste.

```tsx id="u6e3m5"
const patients = [

    name: "Krishna",
    bed: "BED-10",
    ward: "ICU Zenith",
    status: "Stable",
  },
];

export default function BioDashboard() {

  const getStatusColor = (status:string) => {

    if(status === "Critical"){
      return {
        bg:"rgba(255,77,109,0.14)",
        text:"#ff4d6d"
      };
    }

    if(status === "Observation"){
      return {
        bg:"rgba(255,152,0,0.14)",
        text:"#ff9800"
      };
    }

    return {
      bg:"rgba(52,197,107,0.14)",
      text:"#34C56B"
    };
  };

  return (

    <div
      style={{
        minHeight:"100vh",
        background:
        "linear-gradient(to bottom right,#F4EEF7,#F7F1FF)",
        padding:"40px",
        fontFamily:"Inter,sans-serif"
      }}
    >

      {/* TOPBAR */}

      <motion.div

        initial={{
          opacity:0,
          y:-30
        }}

        animate={{
          opacity:1,
          y:0
        }}

        transition={{
          duration:1
        }}

        style={{
          marginBottom:"50px",
          display:"flex",
          justifyContent:"space-between",
          alignItems:"center"
        }}
      >

        <div>

          <h1
            style={{
              fontSize:"58px",
              color:"#7A36F0",
              margin:0,
              fontWeight:"800"
            }}
          >
            VI-TRAK 637
          </h1>

          <p
            style={{
              color:"#777",
              marginTop:"10px",
              fontSize:"18px",
              letterSpacing:"1px"
            }}
          >
            Advanced Intelligent Bio Monitoring System
          </p>

        </div>

      </motion.div>

      {/* GRID */}

      <div
        style={{
          display:"grid",
          gridTemplateColumns:
          "repeat(auto-fit,minmax(290px,1fr))",
          gap:"28px"
        }}
      >

        {patients.map((p,index)=>(

          <motion.div

            key={index}

            whileHover={{
              y:-8,
              scale:1.02
            }}

            initial={{
              opacity:0,
              y:30
            }}

            animate={{
              opacity:1,
              y:0
            }}

            transition={{
              delay:index * 0.05
            }}

            style={{
              minHeight:"420px",
              borderRadius:"42px",
              padding:"28px",

              background:
              "rgba(255,255,255,0.55)",

              backdropFilter:"blur(24px)",

              border:
              "2px solid rgba(186,104,255,0.20)",

              boxShadow:
              "0 10px 40px rgba(180,120,255,0.10)"
            }}
          >

            <div
              style={{
                display:"flex",
                justifyContent:"space-between",
                alignItems:"center"
              }}
            >

              <div>

                <h1
                  style={{
                    margin:0,
                    fontSize:"34px"
                  }}
                >
                  {p.name}
                </h1>

                <p
                  style={{
                    marginTop:"8px",
                    color:"#888"
                  }}
                >
                  {p.ward}
                </p>

              </div>

              <div
                style={{
                  padding:"10px 18px",
                  borderRadius:"30px",

                  background:
                  getStatusColor(p.status).bg,

                  color:
                  getStatusColor(p.status).text,

                  fontWeight:"800"
                }}
              >
                ● {p.status}
              </div>

            </div>

            <div
              style={{
                marginTop:"40px"
              }}
            >

              <h2
                style={{
                  color:"#A855F7"
                }}
              >
                {p.bed}
              </h2>

            </div>

            <div
              style={{
                marginTop:"40px",
                padding:"20px",
                borderRadius:"24px",

                background:
                "rgba(255,255,255,0.5)",

                color:"#777",

                fontWeight:"700"
              }}
            >
              🛡 NOMINAL • NO ACTIVE ALERTS
            </div>

            <motion.button

              whileHover={{
                scale:1.05
              }}

              whileTap={{
                scale:0.96
              }}

              style={{
                marginTop:"40px",
                border:"none",

                padding:"18px 28px",

                borderRadius:"24px",

                background:
                "rgba(255,255,255,0.75)",

                fontWeight:"800",

                fontSize:"18px",

                color:"#64748B",

                cursor:"pointer"
              }}
            >
              Export CSV
            </motion.button>

          </motion.div>

        ))}

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
const exportCSV = (patient:any) => {

const csv =

`Name,Bed,Ward,Status,HR,RR,SpO2,Temp,PR\n` +

`${patient.name},
${patient.bed},
${patient.ward},
${patient.status},
${patient.hr},

});

link.href = url;

link.download =
`${patient.name}_telemetry.csv`;

link.click();

URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
};
```

# 2️⃣ `src/components/BioDashboard.tsx`

```tsx
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { patients } from "../data/patients";

export default function BioDashboard(){

const exportCSV = (patient:any) => {

const csv =

`Name,Bed,Ward,Status,HR,RR,SpO2,Temp,PR\n` +

`${patient.name},
${patient.bed},
${patient.ward},
${patient.status},
${patient.hr},
${patient.rr},
${patient.spo2},
${patient.temp},
${patient.pr}`;

const blob = new Blob([csv], {
type:"text/csv;charset=utf-8;"
});

const link = document.createElement("a");

const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

link.href = url;

link.download =
`${patient.name}_telemetry.csv`;

link.click();

URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
};

const getColor = (condition:string)=>{

if(condition==="Critical") return "#ff4d6d";

if(condition==="Observation") return "#ff9800";

if(condition==="Disconnected") return "#b8b8c7";

return "#34C56B";
};

return(

<div
style={{
minHeight:"100vh",
padding:"40px",
background:"#F4EEF7",
fontFamily:"Inter,sans-serif"
}}
>

<div
style={{
marginBottom:"40px"
}}
>

<h1
style={{
fontSize:"56px",
fontFamily:"Orbitron",
color:"#7A36F0"
}}
>
VI-TRAK 637
</h1>

<p
style={{
marginTop:"10px",
color:"#777",
fontWeight:"500"
}}
>
Advanced Human Bio Analytics Engine
</p>

</div>

<div
style={{
display:"grid",
gridTemplateColumns:"repeat(5,1fr)",
gap:"26px"
}}
>

{patients.map((p,index)=>(

<motion.div

key={index}

initial={{
opacity:0,
y:30
}}

animate={{
opacity:1,
y:0
}}

transition={{
delay:index*0.05
}}

whileHover={{
y:-8,
scale:1.02
}}

style={{
background:"rgba(255,255,255,0.65)",
backdropFilter:"blur(20px)",

borderRadius:"38px",

padding:"24px",

border:"2px solid rgba(203,108,230,0.28)",

boxShadow:
"0 8px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)"
}}
>

<div
style={{
display:"flex",
justifyContent:"space-between",
alignItems:"center"
}}
>

<div
style={{
display:"flex",
alignItems:"center",
gap:"14px"
}}
>

<div
style={{
width:"58px",
height:"58px",
borderRadius:"50%",
background:"rgba(203,108,230,0.12)",

display:"flex",
justifyContent:"center",
alignItems:"center",

fontSize:"26px",
color:"#CB6CE6"
}}
>
◉
</div>

<div>

<h2
style={{
fontSize:"28px",
fontWeight:"700"
}}
>
{p.name}
</h2>

<p
style={{
marginTop:"3px",
fontSize:"13px",
color:"#888"
}}
>
VT-00{p.id}
</p>

</div>

</div>

<div
style={{
textAlign:"right"
}}
>

<div
style={{
fontSize:"14px",
fontWeight:"700",
color:"#B36BFF"
}}
>
{p.bed}
</div>

<p
style={{
fontSize:"12px",
marginTop:"6px",
color:"#999"
}}
>
{p.ward}
</p>

</div>

</div>

<div
style={{
marginTop:"26px",
display:"flex",
justifyContent:"space-between",
alignItems:"center"
}}
>

<span
style={{
fontSize:"13px",
letterSpacing:"2px",
color:"#777",
fontWeight:"700"
}}
>
CONDITION
</span>

<div
style={{
padding:"8px 16px",
borderRadius:"30px",

background:
`${getColor(p.status)}22`,

color:getColor(p.status),

fontSize:"12px",
fontWeight:"700"
}}
>
● {p.status.toUpperCase()}
</div>

</div>

<div
style={{
marginTop:"30px",
display:"flex",
flexDirection:"column",
gap:"20px"
}}
>

<Metric
label="HR"
value={p.hr}
unit="BPM"
color="#ff4d6d"
/>

<Metric
label="RR"
value={p.rr}
unit="/min"
color="#4d96ff"
/>

<Metric
label="SpO₂"
value={p.spo2}
unit="%"
color="#34C56B"
/>

<Metric
label="TEMP"
value={p.temp}
unit="°C"
color="#ff9800"
/>

<Metric
label="PR"
value={p.pr}
unit="BPM"
color="#CB6CE6"
/>

</div>

<div
style={{
marginTop:"28px",
padding:"16px",
borderRadius:"22px",

background:
p.connected
? "rgba(52,197,107,0.08)"
: "rgba(180,180,190,0.08)",

color:
p.connected
? "#34C56B"
: "#999",

fontWeight:"700",
fontSize:"13px",

display:"flex",
justifyContent:"center"
}}
>
{p.connected
? "CONNECTED • LIVE"
: "NOT CONNECTED"}
</div>

<div
style={{
marginTop:"30px",
display:"flex",
justifyContent:"space-between",
alignItems:"center"
}}
>

<div>

<p
style={{
fontSize:"12px",
color:"#9AA0B5",
fontWeight:"700"
}}
>
DATA PACKET
</p>

<p
style={{
marginTop:"8px",
fontSize:"13px",
fontWeight:"700",
color:
p.connected
? "#34C56B"
: "#999"
}}
>
{p.connected
? "● LIVE"
: "● OFFLINE"}
</p>

</div>

<button

onClick={() => exportCSV(p)}

style={{
border:"none",
padding:"16px 24px",
borderRadius:"22px",

background:"rgba(255,255,255,0.7)",

fontWeight:"700",
cursor:"pointer",

color:"#7A36F0"
}}
>
Export CSV
</button>

</div>

</motion.div>

))}

</div>

</div>

)

}

function Metric({
label,
value,
unit,
color
}:any){

return(

<div
style={{
display:"flex",
justifyContent:"space-between",
alignItems:"center"
}}
>

<div
style={{
display:"flex",
alignItems:"center",
gap:"12px"
}}
>

<div
style={{
width:"12px",
height:"12px",
borderRadius:"50%",
background:color
}}
/>

<span
style={{
fontWeight:"700",
color:"#666"
}}
>
{label}
</span>

</div>

<div
style={{
display:"flex",
alignItems:"center",
gap:"8px"
}}
>

<span
style={{
fontSize:"40px",
fontWeight:"800",
color:"#14141A"
}}
>
{value}
</span>

<span
style={{
fontSize:"12px",
color:"#888"
}}
>
{unit}
</span>

</div>

</div>

)

}
```

# 3️⃣ `src/App.tsx`

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

import IntroScreen from "./components/IntroScreen";
import BioDashboard from "./components/BioDashboard";

function App(){

const [loading,setLoading] = useState(true);

useEffect(()=>{

setTimeout(()=>{

setLoading(false);

},5000);

},[]);

return(

<>

{loading
? <IntroScreen/>
: <BioDashboard/>
}

</>

)

}

export default App;
```

# 🔥 STEP 5 — REPLACE `src/components/BioDashboard.tsx`

```tsx id="c6f1y9"
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { patients } from "../data/patients";

export default function BioDashboard(){

const exportCSV = (patient:any) => {

const csv =

`Name,Bed,Ward,Status,HR,RR,SpO2,Temp,PR\n` +

`${patient.name},
${patient.bed},
${patient.ward},
${patient.status},
${patient.hr},
${patient.rr},
${patient.spo2},
${patient.temp},
${patient.pr}`;

const blob = new Blob([csv], {
type:"text/csv;charset=utf-8;"
});

const link = document.createElement("a");

const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

link.href = url;

link.download =
`${patient.name}_telemetry.csv`;

link.click();

URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
};

const getColor = (condition:string)=>{

if(condition==="Critical") return "#ff4d6d";

if(condition==="Observation") return "#ff9800";

if(condition==="Disconnected") return "#b8b8c7";

return "#34C56B";
};

return(

<div
style={{
minHeight:"100vh",
padding:"40px",
background:"#F4EEF7",
fontFamily:"Inter,sans-serif"
}}
>

<h1
style={{
fontSize:"56px",
fontFamily:"Orbitron",
color:"#7A36F0",
marginBottom:"10px"
}}
>
VI-TRAK 637
</h1>

<p
style={{
marginBottom:"40px",
color:"#777"
}}
>
Advanced Human Bio Analytics Engine
</p>

<div
style={{
display:"grid",
gridTemplateColumns:"repeat(5,1fr)",
gap:"24px"
}}
>

{patients.map((p,index)=>(

<motion.div

key={index}

whileHover={{
y:-8,
scale:1.02
}}

initial={{
opacity:0,
y:30
}}

animate={{
opacity:1,
y:0
}}

transition={{
delay:index*0.05
}}

style={{
background:"rgba(255,255,255,0.65)",
backdropFilter:"blur(20px)",

borderRadius:"38px",

padding:"24px",

border:"2px solid rgba(203,108,230,0.28)",

boxShadow:
"0 8px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)"
}}
>

<h2>{p.name}</h2>

<p
style={{
marginTop:"4px",
color:"#999"
}}
>
VT-00{p.id}
</p>

<div
style={{

}}
>
{p.bed}
</div>

<div
style={{

borderRadius:"30px",

background:
`${getColor(p.status)}22`,

color:getColor(p.status),

fontSize:"12px",
fontWeight:"700",

width:"fit-content"
}}
>
● {p.status.toUpperCase()}
</div>

<div
style={{
marginTop:"24px",
display:"flex",
flexDirection:"column",
gap:"18px"
}}
>

<Metric label="HR" value={p.hr} unit="BPM"/>
<Metric label="RR" value={p.rr} unit="/min"/>
<Metric label="SpO₂" value={p.spo2} unit="%"/>
<Metric label="TEMP" value={p.temp} unit="°C"/>
<Metric label="PR" value={p.pr} unit="BPM"/>

</div>

<div
style={{
marginTop:"24px",
padding:"16px",
borderRadius:"20px",

background:
p.connected
? "rgba(52,197,107,0.08)"
: "rgba(180,180,190,0.08)",

color:
p.connected
? "#34C56B"
: "#999",

fontWeight:"700",
textAlign:"center"
}}
>
{p.connected
? "CONNECTED • LIVE"
: "NOT CONNECTED"}
</div>

<button

onClick={() => exportCSV(p)}

style={{
marginTop:"24px",
width:"100%",
border:"none",
padding:"16px",
borderRadius:"20px",

background:"rgba(255,255,255,0.7)",

fontWeight:"700",

cursor:"pointer",

color:"#7A36F0"
}}
>
Export CSV
</button>

</motion.div>

))}

</div>

</div>

)

}

function Metric({
label,
value,
unit
}:any){

return(

<div
style={{
display:"flex",
justifyContent:"space-between",
alignItems:"center"
}}
>

<span
style={{
fontWeight:"700",
color:"#666"
}}
>
{label}
</span>

<div
style={{
display:"flex",
alignItems:"center",
gap:"8px"
}}
>

<span
style={{
fontSize:"34px",
fontWeight:"800"
}}
>
{value}
</span>

<span
style={{
fontSize:"12px",
color:"#888"
}}
>
{unit}
</span>

</div>

</div>

)

}
```

# 🔥 STEP 4 — SENSOR CONNECTIONS

---

# MAX30102 → ESP32

```text id="g9w6m2"
VIN  → 3.3V
GND  → GND
SCL  → GPIO22
SDA  → GPIO21
```

---

# DS18B20 → ESP32

```text id="m5q8z7"
VCC → 3.3V
GND → GND
DATA → GPIO4
```

Add:

```text id="t7n2v5"
4.7k resistor
```

between:

```text id="h1x6c8"
DATA ↔ VCC
```

---

# 🔥 STEP 5 — TEST MAX30102

Upload THIS:

```cpp id="z4r7m1"
#include <Wire.h>
#include "MAX30105.h"

MAX30105 sensor;

void setup() {

Serial.begin(115200);

if(!sensor.begin(Wire)){
Serial.println("MAX30102 NOT FOUND");
while(1);
}

Serial.println("MAX30102 READY");
}

void loop(){

long irValue = sensor.getIR();

Serial.println(irValue);

delay(100);
}
```

# 🔥 STEP 13 — ESP32 CLOUD CODE

```cpp id="k7n4v1"
#include <WiFi.h>
#include <Firebase_ESP_Client.h>

#define WIFI_SSID "YOUR_WIFI"
#define WIFI_PASSWORD "YOUR_PASS"

#define API_KEY "YOUR_FIREBASE_API_KEY"
#define DATABASE_URL "YOUR_DB_URL"

FirebaseData fbdo;
FirebaseAuth auth;
FirebaseConfig config;

void setup(){

Serial.begin(115200);

WiFi.begin(WIFI_SSID,WIFI_PASSWORD);

while(WiFi.status()!=WL_CONNECTED){
delay(1000);
}

config.api_key = API_KEY;
config.database_url = DATABASE_URL;

Firebase.begin(&config,&auth);
Firebase.reconnectWiFi(true);
}

void loop(){

int hr = random(70,90);
int spo2 = random(95,100);
float temp = 36.6;

Firebase.RTDB.setInt(&fbdo,
"/patient1/hr",hr);

Firebase.RTDB.setInt(&fbdo,
"/patient1/spo2",spo2);

Firebase.RTDB.setFloat(&fbdo,
"/patient1/temp",temp);

delay(1000);
}
```

```tsx id="x2r8v6"
} from "firebase/firestore";
```

```tsx id="m1q5z9"
useEffect(()=>{

const unsub = onSnapshot(
doc(db,"patients","patient1"),

(doc)=>{

const data = doc.data();

console.log(data);

});

return ()=>unsub();

},[]);
```

# 🔥 STEP 10 — ESP32 CLOUD CODE

```cpp id="w6k2n4"
#include <WiFi.h>
#include <Firebase_ESP_Client.h>

#define WIFI_SSID "YOUR_WIFI"
#define WIFI_PASSWORD "YOUR_PASSWORD"

#define API_KEY "YOUR_FIREBASE_API_KEY"

#define DATABASE_URL
"https://YOUR_PROJECT.firebaseio.com/"

FirebaseData fbdo;
FirebaseAuth auth;
FirebaseConfig config;

void setup(){

Serial.begin(115200);

WiFi.begin(WIFI_SSID,WIFI_PASSWORD);

while(WiFi.status()!=WL_CONNECTED){
delay(1000);
}

config.api_key = API_KEY;
config.database_url = DATABASE_URL;

Firebase.begin(&config,&auth);

Firebase.reconnectWiFi(true);
}

void loop(){

int hr = random(70,90);

int spo2 = random(95,100);

float temp = 36.6;

Firebase.RTDB.setInt(
&fbdo,
"/patient1/hr",
hr
);

Firebase.RTDB.setInt(
&fbdo,
"/patient1/spo2",
spo2
);

Firebase.RTDB.setFloat(
&fbdo,
"/patient1/temp",
temp
);

delay(1000);
}
```

```js id="y3w8m1"
const express = require("express");

const cors = require("cors");

const { SerialPort } =
require("serialport");

const { ReadlineParser } =
require("@serialport/parser-readline");

const app = express();

app.use(cors());

let latestData = {

hr: "--",
spo2: "--",
temp: "--",
rr: "--"

};

const port = new SerialPort({

path: "COM5",
baudRate: 115200

});

const parser =
port.pipe(new ReadlineParser({
delimiter: "\r\n"
}));

parser.on("data",(data)=>{

console.log("RAW:",data);

const values = data.split(",");

latestData = {

hr: values[0],
spo2: values[1],
temp: values[2],
rr: values[3]

};

});

app.get("/data",(req,res)=>{

res.json(latestData);

});

app.listen(5000,()=>{

console.log(
"SERVER RUNNING ON PORT 5000"
);

});
```

# 🧠 WHAT MQ2 DETECTS

MQ2 detects:

✅ LPG  
✅ Propane  
✅ Methane  
✅ Smoke  
✅ Hydrogen  

Best for:

✅ gas leakage monitoring  
✅ safety systems  
✅ kitchen monitoring  
✅ industrial alerts  

---

# 🔌 MQ2 PINOUT

Most MQ2 modules have:

```text id="a1m8x4"
VCC
GND
A0
D0
```

---

# 🔥 ESP32 ↔ MQ2 CONNECTION

| MQ2 | ESP32 |
|---|---|
| VCC | 5V |
| GND | GND |
| A0 | GPIO34 |
| D0 | OPTIONAL |

---

# ⚠ IMPORTANT

Use:

```text id="q7n2v5"
A0
```

NOT D0.

Because:

✅ A0 gives analog gas levels  
✅ more accurate readings  
✅ allows percentage calculation  

---

# 🔥 WIRING DIAGRAM

```text id="f4x9m1"
MQ2         ESP32

VCC   →     5V
GND   →     GND
A0    →     GPIO34
```

---

```cpp id="k8w3r6"
Serial.println(gasValue);

delay(1000);

}
```
